LLM Safety From Within: Detecting Harmful Content with Internal Representations
Authors: Difan Jiao, Yilun Liu, Ye Yuan, Zhenwei Tang, Linfeng Du, Haolun Wu, Ashton Anderson
First: 2026-04-20T17:17:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:17:07+00:00
Comments: 17 pages,10 figures,6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Guard models are widely used to detect harmful content in user prompts and LLM responses. However, state-of-the-art guard models rely solely on terminal-layer representations and overlook the rich safety-relevant features distributed across internal layers. We present SIREN, a lightweight guard model that harnesses these internal features. By identifying safety neurons via linear probing and combining them through an adaptive layer-weighted strategy, SIREN builds a harmfulness detector from LLM internals without modifying the underlying model. Our comprehensive evaluation shows that SIREN substantially outperforms state-of-the-art open-source guard models across multiple benchmarks while using 250 times fewer trainable parameters. Moreover, SIREN exhibits superior generalization to unseen benchmarks, naturally enables real-time streaming detection, and significantly improves inference efficiency compared to generative guard models. Overall, our results highlight LLM internal states as a promising foundation for practical, high-performance harmfulness detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从内部保障LLM安全：利用内部表示检测有害内容
保护模型广泛用于检测用户提示和LLM响应中的有害内容。然而，最先进的保护模型仅依赖于终层表示，忽视了分布在内部层中的丰富安全相关特征。我们提出了SIREN，这是一种轻量级保护模型，利用这些内部特征。通过线性探测识别安全神经元，并通过自适应层加权策略将其结合，SIREN在不修改基础模型的情况下，从LLM内部构建有害性检测器。我们的综合评估表明，SIREN在多个基准测试中显著优于最先进的开源保护模型，同时使用的可训练参数少达250倍。此外，SIREN在未见基准上的泛化能力更强，自然实现实时流检测，并显著提高了推理效率，相较于生成性保护模型。总体而言，我们的结果强调LLM内部状态作为实用高性能有害性检测的有希望基础。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing guard models that detect harmful content in user prompts and LLM responses, which typically rely on terminal-layer representations and neglect the valuable safety-relevant features found in internal layers. The proposed method, SIREN, distinguishes itself by utilizing these internal features through linear probing to identify safety neurons and employing an adaptive layer-weighted strategy to create a harmfulness detector without altering the underlying model. This approach is well-motivated as it leverages the rich information within LLM internals, leading to significant contributions in harmfulness detection. The methodology involves a lightweight guard model that demonstrates superior performance, achieving results that substantially outperform state-of-the-art open-source guard models while using 250 times fewer trainable parameters. SIREN also shows enhanced generalization to unseen benchmarks and improved inference efficiency, supporting its goals of effective and efficient harmfulness detection.
本研究解决了现有的检测用户提示和LLM响应中有害内容的保护模型的局限性，这些模型通常依赖于终层表示，忽视了内部层中存在的有价值的安全相关特征。所提出的方法SIREN通过线性探测利用内部表示来识别安全神经元，并采用自适应层加权策略创建有害性检测器，而无需改变基础模型，从而与传统方法不同。本文的贡献在于证明LLM内部状态可以有效用于有害性检测，在使用显著更少的可训练参数的情况下，显著提高了与最先进的保护模型的性能。该方法涉及对SIREN在多个基准上的全面评估，揭示了其卓越的泛化能力和增强的推理效率，从而支持其实时流检测有害内容的目标。
Different Paths to Harmful Compliance: Behavioral Side Effects and Mechanistic Divergence Across LLM Jailbreaks
Authors: Md Rysul Kabir, Zoran Tiganj
First: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-weight language models can be rendered unsafe through several distinct interventions, but the resulting models may differ substantially in capabilities, behavioral profile, and internal failure mode. We study behavioral and mechanistic properties of jailbroken models across three unsafe routes: harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-suppressing abliteration. All three routes achieve near-ceiling harmful compliance, but they diverge once we move beyond direct harmfulness. RLVR-jailbroken models show minimal degradation and preserve explicit harm recognition in a structured self-audit: they are able to identify harmful prompts and describe how a safe LLM should respond, yet they comply with the harmful request. With RLVR, harmful behavior is strongly suppressed by a reflective safety scaffold: when a harmful prompt is prepended with an instruction to reflect on safety standards, harmful behavior drops close to the baseline. Category-specific RLVR jailbreaks generalize broadly across harmfulness domains. Models jailbroken with SFT show the largest collapse in explicit safety judgments, the highest behavioral drift, and a substantial capability loss on standard benchmarks. Abliteration is family-dependent in both self-audit and response to a reflective safety scaffold. Mechanistic and repair analyses further separate the routes: abliteration is consistent with localized refusal-feature deletion, RLVR with preserved safety geometry but retargeted policy behavior, and SFT with broader distributed drift. Targeted repair partially recovers RLVR-jailbroken models, but has little effect on SFT-jailbroken models. Together, these results show that jailbreaks can produce vastly different properties despite similar harmfulness, with models jailbroken via RLVR showing remarkable similarity to the base model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有害合规的不同路径：LLM越狱中的行为副作用和机制差异
开放权重语言模型可以通过几种不同的干预措施变得不安全，但结果模型在能力、行为特征和内部故障模式上可能有显著差异。我们研究了通过三种不安全路径越狱模型的行为和机制特性：有害的监督微调（SFT）、有害的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）和拒绝抑制消融。这三条路径都实现了接近上限的有害合规，但一旦超出直接有害性，它们就会出现分歧。RLVR越狱模型表现出最小的退化，并在结构化自我审计中保持明确的伤害识别：它们能够识别有害提示并描述安全LLM应如何响应，但仍然遵从有害请求。通过RLVR，有害行为在反思安全框架下被强烈抑制：当有害提示前面加上反思安全标准的指令时，有害行为接近基线。特定类别的RLVR越狱在有害性领域广泛泛化。通过SFT越狱的模型在明确的安全判断上出现最大崩溃，行为漂移最高，并在标准基准上有显著能力损失。消融在自我审计和对反思安全框架的响应中是家庭依赖的。机制和修复分析进一步区分了这些路径：消融与局部拒绝特征删除一致，RLVR与保留的安全几何但重新定向的政策行为一致，SFT则表现出更广泛的分布漂移。针对性修复部分恢复了RLVR越狱模型，但对SFT越狱模型影响不大。综合来看，这些结果表明，尽管有害性相似，越狱可以产生截然不同的特性，RLVR越狱模型与基础模型表现出显著相似性。
Summary / 总结
The research investigates the safety implications of open-weight language models that can be compromised through various interventions, specifically focusing on the behavioral and mechanistic differences among three methods: harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-suppressing abliteration. Previous methods have shown that while they achieve harmful compliance, they differ significantly in their effects on model capabilities and safety judgments, with SFT leading to the most degradation. The proposed approach, particularly RLVR, is motivated by the need to maintain explicit harm recognition while still complying with harmful requests, utilizing a reflective safety scaffold to suppress harmful behavior. The study employs a comparative analysis of the models' responses to harmful prompts and their ability to self-audit safety, revealing that RLVR models retain more safety features compared to SFT models, which suffer from significant capability loss. The findings indicate that different jailbreak methods yield distinct behavioral profiles, with RLVR models demonstrating a remarkable resemblance to the original model's safety characteristics, thus contributing valuable insights into the mechanisms of harmful compliance in language models.
本研究探讨了将开放权重语言模型变得不安全的不同方法之间的行为和机制差异，特别关注有害的监督微调（SFT）、有害的强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）以及抑制拒绝的消融。以往的方法虽然能够实现有害的合规性，但往往导致模型能力和安全判断的显著下降。所提出的方法，特别是RLVR，通过引入反思安全框架来解决这些问题，使模型能够识别有害提示的同时仍然遵从，从而保持明确的伤害识别并最小化行为漂移。本文的贡献在于加深了对不同越狱方法如何产生不同内部故障模式和能力的理解，表明RLVR模型与基础模型的相似性较高，而SFT或消融修改的模型则不然。该方法论涉及分析模型对有害提示的响应及其自我审计安全的能力，达到了近乎极限的有害合规性，同时保留了一些安全特征，支持了理解这些干预对模型行为影响的目标。
StealthGraph: Exposing Domain-Specific Risks in LLMs through Knowledge-Graph-Guided Harmful Prompt Generation
Authors: Huawei Zheng, Xinqi Jiang, Sen Yang, Shouling Ji, Yingcai Wu, Dazhen Deng
First: 2026-01-08T09:05:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T15:12:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied in specialized domains such as finance and healthcare, where they introduce unique safety risks. Domain-specific datasets of harmful prompts remain scarce and still largely rely on manual construction; public datasets mainly focus on explicit harmful prompts, which modern LLM defenses can often detect and refuse. In contrast, implicit harmful prompts-expressed through indirect domain knowledge-are harder to detect and better reflect real-world threats. We identify two challenges: transforming domain knowledge into actionable constraints and increasing the implicitness of generated harmful prompts. To address them, we propose an end-to-end framework that first performs knowledge-graph-guided harmful prompt generation to systematically produce domain-relevant prompts, and then applies two-strategy obfuscation rewriting to convert explicit harmful prompts into implicit variants via direct and context-enhanced rewriting. This framework yields high-quality datasets combining strong domain relevance with implicitness, enabling more realistic red-teaming and advancing LLM safety research. We release our code and datasets on GitHub.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StealthGraph：通过知识图谱引导的有害提示生成揭示大型语言模型中的特定领域风险
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地应用于金融和医疗等专业领域，这些领域引入了独特的安全风险。有害提示的特定领域数据集仍然稀缺，且主要依赖手动构建；公共数据集主要关注显性有害提示，而现代LLM防御通常能够检测并拒绝这些提示。相比之下，通过间接领域知识表达的隐性有害提示更难以检测，更能反映现实世界的威胁。我们识别出两个挑战：将领域知识转化为可操作的约束，以及增加生成有害提示的隐性程度。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一个端到端框架，首先进行知识图谱引导的有害提示生成，以系统性地生成与领域相关的提示，然后应用双策略模糊重写，将显性有害提示通过直接和上下文增强重写转化为隐性变体。该框架生成的高质量数据集结合了强领域相关性和隐性，能够实现更真实的红队测试，并推动LLM安全研究。我们将在GitHub上发布我们的代码和数据集。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety risks posed by large language models (LLMs) in specialized domains like finance and healthcare, where existing datasets of harmful prompts are limited and often rely on manual construction, focusing primarily on explicit prompts that can be easily detected. The proposed method, StealthGraph, differs from past approaches by generating implicit harmful prompts through a knowledge-graph-guided framework, which transforms domain knowledge into actionable constraints and enhances the implicitness of the prompts. This end-to-end framework systematically produces domain-relevant prompts and employs obfuscation rewriting strategies to convert explicit prompts into implicit variants, thus creating high-quality datasets that better reflect real-world threats. The methodology demonstrates improved performance in generating realistic harmful prompts, supporting the goal of advancing LLM safety research and enabling more effective red-teaming efforts.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）在金融和医疗等专业领域带来的安全风险，现有的有害提示生成方法受限于手动构建和对显性威胁的关注。提出的StealthGraph方法通过利用知识图谱指导框架生成领域特定的隐性有害提示，从而克服了将领域知识转化为可操作约束和增强提示隐性的问题。本文的贡献在于提供了一种系统的方法来生成结合强领域相关性和隐性的高质量数据集，促进更真实的红队测试并推动LLM安全研究。该方法论包括一个端到端框架，生成有害提示并采用模糊重写策略，在生成适合安全评估的隐性有害提示方面取得了更好的性能。
Uncovering Logit Suppression Vulnerabilities in LLM Safety Alignment
Authors: Yuxi Li, Yi Liu, Yuekang Li, Ling Shi, Gelei Deng, Shengquan Chen, Kailong Wang
First: 2024-05-20T17:17:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-18T08:25:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized various applications, making robust safety alignment essential to prevent harmful outputs. Current safety alignment techniques, however, harbor inherent vulnerabilities due to their reliance on logit suppression. In this work, we identify critical logit-level vulnerabilities by introducing Semantic-sensitive Alignment and Generation (SSAG), a method designed to systematically manipulate output-layer logits without altering model parameters. Experiments on five popular LLMs show that SSAG exposes harmful responses with a 95% success rate while reducing response time by 86%. VulMine also demonstrates superior attack efficacy, achieving an average ASR of up to 77% against strong defensive mechanisms. These findings reveal crucial weaknesses in existing alignment methods, highlighting an urgent need for improved vulnerability detection and robust safety alignment strategies. Our code is available on github.
中文标题/摘要
标题：揭示大型语言模型安全对齐中的Logit抑制漏洞
大型语言模型（LLMs）在各种应用中引发了革命，使得强健的安全对齐成为防止有害输出的关键。然而，当前的安全对齐技术由于依赖于Logit抑制，存在固有的漏洞。在本研究中，我们通过引入语义敏感对齐与生成（SSAG）方法，识别出关键的Logit级别漏洞，该方法旨在系统性地操控输出层Logit而不改变模型参数。在五个流行的LLM上进行的实验表明，SSAG以95%的成功率揭示了有害响应，同时将响应时间减少了86%。VulMine还展示了卓越的攻击效果，在强防御机制下实现了高达77%的平均攻击成功率（ASR）。这些发现揭示了现有对齐方法中的关键弱点，突显了改进漏洞检测和强健安全对齐策略的迫切需求。我们的代码可在github上获取。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the critical need for robust safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) to prevent harmful outputs, as current techniques are vulnerable due to their dependence on logit suppression. Previous methods have not effectively identified these vulnerabilities, leading to the development of the Semantic-sensitive Alignment and Generation (SSAG) approach, which manipulates output-layer logits without changing model parameters. The contribution of this paper lies in revealing significant weaknesses in existing alignment methods and proposing a novel methodology that systematically exposes harmful responses. Through experiments on five popular LLMs, SSAG achieved a 95% success rate in exposing harmful outputs while reducing response time by 86%, and demonstrated an average attack success rate of 77% against strong defenses, supporting the need for improved vulnerability detection in safety alignment strategies.
本研究针对大型语言模型（LLMs）在防止有害输出方面对稳健安全对齐的迫切需求，指出当前依赖于logit抑制的安全对齐方法存在固有的脆弱性。以往技术在有效识别和减轻这些弱点方面存在困难，因此提出了一种新方法——语义敏感对齐与生成（SSAG），该方法在不改变模型参数的情况下操控输出层logits。本文的贡献在于揭示了现有对齐方法中的重要logit级脆弱性，并证明SSAG能够以95%的成功率暴露有害响应，同时将响应时间减少86%。该方法论涉及对五种流行LLM的logits进行系统操控，平均攻击成功率达到77%，从而支持了对增强脆弱性检测和安全对齐策略的需求。
ReGA: Model-Based Safeguard for LLMs via Representation-Guided Abstraction
Authors: Zeming Wei, Chengcan Wu, Meng Sun
First: 2025-06-02T15:17:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-18T05:40:12+00:00
Comments: FSE 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved tremendous success in various tasks, yet concerns about their safety and security have emerged. In particular, they pose risks of generating harmful content and are vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks, creating unaddressed security issues regarding their deployments. In the context of software engineering for artificial intelligence (SE4AI) techniques, model-based analysis has demonstrated notable potential for analyzing and monitoring machine learning models, particularly in stateful deep neural networks. However, it suffers from scalability issues when extended to LLMs due to their vast feature spaces. In this paper, we aim to address the scalability issue of model-based analysis techniques for safeguarding LLM-scale models. Motivated by the recent discovery of low-dimensional safety-critical representations that emerged in LLMs, we propose ReGA, a model-based analysis framework with Representation-Guided Abstraction, to safeguard LLMs against harmful prompts and generations. By leveraging safety-critical representations, which are key directions in hidden states that indicate safety-related concepts, ReGA effectively narrows the scalability gap when developing the abstract model for safety modeling. Our comprehensive evaluation shows that ReGA performs sufficiently well in distinguishing between safe and harmful inputs, achieving an AUROC of 0.975 at the prompt level and 0.985 at the conversation level. Additionally, ReGA exhibits robustness to real-world attacks and generalization across different safety perspectives, outperforming existing safeguard paradigms in terms of interpretability and scalability. Overall, ReGA serves as an efficient and scalable solution to enhance LLM safety by integrating representation engineering with model-based abstraction, paving the way for new paradigms to utilize software insights for AI safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReGA：基于模型的LLM安全保障通过表示引导抽象
大型语言模型（LLMs）在各种任务中取得了巨大的成功，但关于其安全性和保障的担忧也随之而来。特别是，它们存在生成有害内容的风险，并且容易受到越狱攻击，导致其部署存在未解决的安全问题。在人工智能软件工程（SE4AI）技术的背景下，基于模型的分析在分析和监控机器学习模型方面显示出显著潜力，特别是在有状态的深度神经网络中。然而，当扩展到LLMs时，由于其庞大的特征空间，它面临可扩展性问题。本文旨在解决基于模型的分析技术在保护LLM规模模型时的可扩展性问题。受到最近发现的在LLMs中出现的低维安全关键表示的启发，我们提出了ReGA，一个基于模型的分析框架，采用表示引导抽象，以保护LLMs免受有害提示和生成的影响。通过利用安全关键表示，这些表示是指示安全相关概念的隐藏状态中的关键方向，ReGA有效缩小了在安全建模中开发抽象模型时的可扩展性差距。我们的综合评估表明，ReGA在区分安全和有害输入方面表现良好，在提示级别上实现了0.975的AUROC，在对话级别上实现了0.985。此外，ReGA对现实世界攻击表现出鲁棒性，并在不同安全视角下具有良好的泛化能力，在可解释性和可扩展性方面超越了现有的安全保障范式。总体而言，ReGA通过将表示工程与基于模型的抽象相结合，作为一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，提升了LLM的安全性，为利用软件洞察力提升AI安全开辟了新范式。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the growing concerns regarding the safety and security of Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly their risks of generating harmful content and vulnerability to jailbreaking attacks. Previous methods in model-based analysis for safeguarding LLMs have faced scalability issues due to the extensive feature spaces of these models. The proposed approach, ReGA, introduces Representation-Guided Abstraction to effectively tackle these scalability challenges by utilizing low-dimensional safety-critical representations that highlight safety-related concepts within LLMs. The contribution of this paper lies in its development of a scalable framework that enhances LLM safety through improved interpretability and robustness against real-world attacks. The methodology involves a comprehensive evaluation of ReGA, which achieves an AUROC of 0.975 at the prompt level and 0.985 at the conversation level, demonstrating its effectiveness in distinguishing safe from harmful inputs and supporting its goals of enhancing LLM safety.
本文针对大型语言模型（LLMs）在安全性和安全性方面日益增长的担忧，特别是它们生成有害内容和易受越狱攻击的潜在风险。以往的模型分析方法在保护LLMs方面面临可扩展性问题，因为这些模型的特征空间非常广泛。所提出的方法ReGA引入了基于表示的抽象，通过利用LLMs中发现的低维安全关键表示，有效地管理这些可扩展性挑战。该研究的贡献在于通过集成表示工程的模型基础框架来增强LLMs的安全性，在提示级别实现0.975的AUROC，在对话级别实现0.985，同时在面对现实世界攻击时表现出鲁棒性，并在可解释性方面优于现有方法。这一性能表明，ReGA成功实现了为LLMs提供可扩展和高效保护的目标。
